    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        for i in $(seq 1 24); do
          output=$(ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null           -o ConnectTimeout=5 -o BatchMode=yes -o LogLevel=ERROR           admin@172.16.2.23 echo ok 2>&1)

TASK [sonic_fabric : apply | Configure FRR BGP via vtysh (fabric_mode=incremental)] *****************************************************************************************************************************
changed: [Leaf_L2] =>
    attempts: 1
    changed: true
    cmd: |-
        # Remove any existing BGP instance running under a DIFFERENT ASN.
        old_asn=$(docker exec bgp vtysh -c 'show running-config' 2>/dev/null          | awk '/^router bgp/{print $3; exit}')
        if [ -n "$old_asn" ] && [ "$old_asn" != "65012" ]; then
          docker exec bgp vtysh    -c "configure terminal"    -c "no router bgp $old_asn"    -c "end"
        fi

        # Apply BGP config with unnumbered (spine-leaf), IP-based (exit), and downlink (server) peers.
        docker exec bgp vtysh  -c "configure terminal"  -c "router bgp 65012"  -c "bgp router-id 10.0.1.2"  -c "no bgp default ipv4-unicast"  -c "no bgp ebgp-requires-policy"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 remote-as external"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 description Spine_S2"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 remote-asexternal"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 description Spine_S1"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 interface remote-as 65101"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 description Host12_1"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 interface remote-as 65102"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 description Host12_2"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 interface remote-as 65103"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 description Host12_3"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 bfd"  -c "address-family ipv4 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "address-family ipv6 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "end"  -c "write"
    delta: '0:00:00.514978'
    end: '2026-05-30 20:45:47.938249'
    msg: ''
    rc: 0
    start: '2026-05-30 20:45:47.423271'
    stderr: ''
    stderr_lines: <omitted>
    stdout: |-

        Building Configuration...

TASK [sonic_fabric : apply | Configure FRR BGP via vtysh (fabric_mode=incremental)] *****************************************************************************************************************************
changed: [Leaf_L1] =>
    attempts: 1
    changed: true
    cmd: |-
        # Remove any existing BGP instance running under a DIFFERENT ASN.
        old_asn=$(docker exec bgp vtysh -c 'show running-config' 2>/dev/null          | awk '/^router bgp/{print $3; exit}')
        if [ -n "$old_asn" ] && [ "$old_asn" != "65011" ]; then
          docker exec bgp vtysh    -c "configure terminal"    -c "no router bgp $old_asn"    -c "end"
        fi

        # Apply BGP config with unnumbered (spine-leaf), IP-based (exit), and downlink (server) peers.
        docker exec bgp vtysh  -c "configure terminal"  -c "router bgp 65011"  -c "bgp router-id 10.0.1.1"  -c "no bgp default ipv4-unicast"  -c "no bgp ebgp-requires-policy"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 remote-as external"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 description Spine_S2"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 interface v6only"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 remote-asexternal"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 description Spine_S1"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 interface remote-as 65101"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 description Host12_1"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 interface remote-as 65102"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 description Host12_2"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 bfd"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 interface remote-as 65103"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 description Host12_3"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 bfd"  -c "address-family ipv4 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet0 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet1 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet2 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "address-family ipv6 unicast"  -c "redistribute connected"  -c "neighbor Ethernet64 activate"  -c "neighbor Ethernet68 activate"  -c "exit-address-family"  -c "end"  -c "write"

TASK [sonic_fabric : verify | Ping peer IPv6 link-local via fabric interface] ***********************************************************************************************************************************
ok: [Leaf_L2] => (item=Ethernet68 → Spine_S1) =>
    ansible_loop_var: item
    changed: false
    cmd: |-
        peer_ll=""
        port="Ethernet68"
        neighbor="Spine_S1"
        if [ -z "$peer_ll" ]; then
          echo "SKIP: peer link-local not yet known for ${port} → ${neighbor} (BGP not Established yet)"
          exit 0
        fi
        if ping6 -c 3 -W 2 -I "$port" "$peer_ll" > /tmp/ping6_${port}.out 2>&1; then
          echo "OK: ping6 ${peer_ll}%${port} → ${neighbor}: 3/3 replies received"
        else
          rtt=$(grep -oP '(?<=time=)\S+' /tmp/ping6_${port}.out | tail -1 || true)
          loss=$(grep -oP '\d+(?=% packet loss)' /tmp/ping6_${port}.out | tail -1 || true)
          echo "FAIL: ping6 ${peer_ll}%${port} → ${neighbor}: ${loss:-100}% packet loss"
          cat /tmp/ping6_${port}.out
        fi
    delta: '0:00:00.005823'
    end: '2026-05-30 20:47:00.210332'
